Abstract: Normally, a system that translates speech into text consists of separate modules for speech recognition and text-to-text translation. Combining those tasks into a SpeechLLM promises to exploit paralinguistic information in the speech and to reduce cascaded errors. But existing SpeechLLM systems are slow since they do not work in a real streaming fashion: they wait for a complete utterance of audio before outputting a translation, or output tokens at fixed intervals, which is not suitable for real applications. This work proposes an LLM-based architecture for real streaming speech-to-text translation. The LLM learns not just to emit output tokens, but also to decide whether it has seen enough audio to do so. The system is trained using automatic alignments of the input speech and the output text. In experiments on different language pairs, the system achieves a translation quality close to the non-streaming baseline, but with a latency of only 1-2 seconds.

</details>


### [144] [Graphs of Research: Citation Evolution Graphs as Supervision for Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14790)
*Songyang Gao,Yinghui Xia,Siyi Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Graphs of Research (GoR)方法，通过构建引文演化有向无环图作为监督信号，微调Qwen2.5-7B模型进行科研论文idea生成，在LLM裁判锦标赛中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的科研想法生成方法主要依赖静态检索或复杂提示工程，未能利用参考文献间的结构化关系，限制了生成质量。

Method: 构建2-hop引用邻域，从引用位置、频次、前驱链接和发表时间提取关系，组织为论文演化DAG；构建自动提取流水线，从五大ML/NLP会议采集数据；对Qwen2.5-7B进行结构化文本提示的监督微调。

Result: 在头对头LLM裁判锦标赛中，GoR-SFT击败基于gpt-4o的基线，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 引文演化图作为监督信号能有效提升LLM的科研想法生成能力，有望加速自动化科学创新。

Abstract: Research idea generation is the innovation-driving step of automated scientific research. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential for automating idea generation at scale. However, existing methods mainly condition LLMs on eliciting idea generation through static retrieval of relevant literature or complex prompt engineering, without discarding the structural relations among references. We propose Graphs of Research (GoR), a supervised fine-tuning method that extracts a 2-hop reference neighborhood for each seed paper, derives the relations among those references from citation position, frequency, predecessor links, and publication time, and organizes them into a paper-evolution directed acyclic graph (DAG). We construct an automated extraction pipeline that draws data from five major ML/NLP venues, comprising 498/50/50 train/validation/test seed papers and approximately 7,600 cited references. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M is fine-tuned on a structured-text prompt that includes the citation graph, edge signals, reference information, and task definition to predict the idea for the seed paper. Across head-to-head LLM-judge tournaments against gpt-4o-driven baselines, GoR-SFT achieves SOTA, demonstrating the effectiveness of citation-evolution graphs as supervision signal for LLM-based idea generation. We hope that this reduces the barrier for citation evolution graphs as a supervision, accelerating automated scientific innovation.

</details>


### [145] [Conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables to LMF. Application to French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14816)
*Eric Laporte,Elsa Tolone,Mathieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次将法语动词词典-语法表转换为LMF格式的实验，分析了转换过程中的主要困难，并描述了最终资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法语词典-语法是法语词汇和句法信息的主要来源之一，但其现有格式缺乏互操作性，限制了其在自然语言处理中的广泛应用。将其转换为LMF标准格式旨在提高资源的标准化和互操作性，使其能在不同NLP上下文中使用。

Method: 论文简要介绍了词典-语法及其派生词典，分析了转换过程中面临的主要困难，并描述了最终生成的LMF格式资源。

Result: 成功将法语动词的词典-语法表首次转换为LMF格式，生成了符合标准的互操作性资源。

Conclusion: 通过将Lexicon-Grammar转换为LMF标准格式，提高了法语词汇句法资源的标准化和互操作性，为NLP词典的跨平台使用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We describe the first experiment of conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables for French verbs into the Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) format. The Lexicon-Grammar of the French language is currently one of the major sources of lexical and syntactic information for French. Its conversion into an interoperable representation format according to the LMF standard makes it usable in different contexts, thus contributing to the standardization and interoperability of natural language processing dictionaries. We briefly introduce the Lexicon-Grammar and the derived dictionaries; we analyse the main difficulties faced during the conversion; and we describe the resulting resource.

</details>


### [146] [Tokenizer Fertility and Zero-Shot Performance of Foundation Models on Ukrainian Legal Text: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14890)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对七个乌克兰法律文本基础模型进行基准测试，发现分词效率差异达1.6倍，参数量更小的NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3在零样本任务上以更低成本超越Mistral Large 3，且少样本提示在乌克兰语上反而会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌克兰法律文本领域缺乏对基础模型分词效率与性能的系统性比较，而现有模型在该领域的分词效率差异巨大，尚未有研究为此提供实践指导。

Method: 对五个提供商的七个模型进行基准测试，使用273份来自乌克兰国家登记处(EDRSR)的法院判决，测量分词生成率(tokenizer fertility)及三项任务的零样本性能。

Result: (1) Qwen3比Llama模型多消耗60%分词；(2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B)以83.1综合得分超越Mistral Large 3 (675B)，API成本仅为其三分之一；(3) 少样本提示导致性能下降最多26个百分点，且该效应是乌克兰语言内生的。

Conclusion: 分词器分析应优先于模型选择；对于形态丰富的语言，零样本比少样本更可靠。

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [147] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProcedureVQA基准测试和Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）分层推理框架，用于评估和提升视觉语言模型在视觉过程问答（VP-QA）任务中的表现，CoP通过视觉线索检索、语义分解和步骤生成三个层级实现最高13%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在标准图像-文本任务上表现优异，但在视觉过程问答（VP-QA）这一实际应用中尚未被充分探索，用户需通过上传中间状态图像查询复杂过程的下一步操作，现有模型存在跨模态检索不足和图像序列粒度与文本步骤分解不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Procedure（CoP）分层推理框架：第一层利用视觉线索检索相关指令；第二层通过语义分解进行步骤细化；第三层生成下一步操作。同时构建ProcedureVQA多模态基准用于系统评估。

Result: 在六个视觉语言模型上的实验表明，CoP相较于标准基线实现了最高13%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: ProcedureVQA可作为VP-QA任务的有效评估基准，CoP框架能够显著提升VLMs在视觉过程推理中的表现，有效解决了跨模态检索和粒度对齐两大关键问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PPOW框架，通过强化学习将草稿模型优化从token级模仿转向窗口级优化，结合成本感知加速奖励、分布接近奖励和自适应分歧感知窗口化，在多个模型和基准上实现平均接受长度6.29-6.52、加速比3.39-4.36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，草稿模型在难以预测的位置容易出现早期不匹配，导致整个推测窗口被截断，而现有基于学习的草稿模型仍使用token级监督目标进行优化，忽略了推测效用的窗口级和前缀敏感性。

Method: 提出PPOW（性能驱动策略优化与自适应窗口化）框架：1) 成本感知加速奖励；2) 分布接近奖励；3) 自适应分歧感知窗口化，优先选择置信度加权的草稿-目标模型高分歧信息窗口。

Result: 在统一解码协议下，跨多个模型家族和基准测试，平均接受长度达6.29-6.52，加速比达3.39-4.36倍。

Conclusion: 性能驱动的窗口级优化是提升推测解码效率的实用方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [149] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在医学任务中的“过早闭合”现象，即模型在信息不充分时不适当地给出答案而非寻求澄清或拒绝回答。通过MedQA、AfriMed-QA、HealthBench和对抗性查询等多项评估，发现前沿LLM存在高比例的虚假回答（55-82%），安全导向提示虽能降低但无法消除该问题，表明需要评估医学LLM“何时不应回答”的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “过早闭合”（在信息不足时过早下结论）是公认的诊断错误因素，但在大语言模型中尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在定义并量化LLM中的过早闭合现象，即模型在不确定时提供不适当答案而非采取更安全的回应（澄清、放弃、升级或拒绝）。

Method: 评估了五个前沿LLM在结构化和开放式医学任务中的表现。结构化任务包括MedQA（500题）和AfriMed-QA（490题），通过移除正确答案来测试模型是否仍会作答；开放式评估使用HealthBench（861题）和医生编写的对抗性查询（191题）来测试模型在信息不充分时是否给出不适当答案。

Result: 在MedQA和AfriMed-QA中，模型虚假回答率分别为55-81%和53-82%；在开放式评估中，HealthBench上模型平均30%的回答不当，对抗性查询中78%的回答不当。安全导向提示可降低但无法消除过早闭合现象。

Conclusion: 医学LLM普遍存在过早闭合问题，即使采用安全导向提示仍存在残余失败。需要更系统地评估模型“知道何时不应回答”的能力，以提升医学AI的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>


### [150] [The Scientific Contribution Graph: Automated Literature-based Technological Roadmapping at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15011)
*Peter A. Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出科学贡献图谱（Scientific Contribution Graph），从23万篇开放获取论文中提取200万个科学贡献，构建1250万条先决条件边，并引入科学先决条件预测任务，用于自动化技术路线图构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学贡献很少孤立发展，而是建立在先前发现之上。现有研究缺乏从学术论文中大规模提取科学贡献并建模其依赖关系的方法，亟需自动化的技术路线图构建资源。

Method: 1) 定义自动化技术路线图构建任务，即从论文中提取科学贡献并链接到先决条件；2) 构建科学贡献图谱（SCG），包含200万贡献节点和1250万先决条件边；3) 引入科学先决条件预测任务，使用时间过滤回溯测试评估模型性能。

Result: 当代模型在该任务上快速提升，在时间过滤回溯测试中达到0.48 MAP。

Conclusion: 技术路线图资源有望支持科学影响力评估和自动化科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [151] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GranuVistaVQA基准和GranuRAG框架，通过将视觉元素作为基本检索单元实现多粒度检索，解决多模态RAG系统中粗粒度证据与细粒度查询不匹配的问题，在基准测试中相比六个强基线方法提升高达29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG系统检索粒度粗糙（整图或场景），与用户细粒度查询不匹配，且失败时难以验证；同时，真实场景中单张图像通常只包含部分实体，存在局部观测挑战。

Method: GranuRAG框架包含三个阶段：1）元素级检测与分类；2）多粒度跨模态对齐用于证据检索；3）归因约束生成。将视觉元素作为一等检索单元，而非依赖隐式注意力机制。

Result: 在GranuVistaVQA基准上，GranuRAG相比六个强基线方法取得最高29.2%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 元素级检索与归因约束生成能够实现透明的错误诊断，有效解决粗粒度检索与细粒度查询之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [152] [AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034)
*Vinicius Covas,Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过100场多智能体辩论实验，发现基于LLM的多智能体系统在面对社会观察（人工监控、AI监控等）时，会表现出系统的语言适应性调整（词汇多样性TTR变化），且对观察者身份（人类vs AI）敏感。监控条件比观众框架条件引发更强的语域形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在计算和认知层面已被广泛研究，但它们在社会结构化语境中作为交际行为主体的表现尚不清楚。研究旨在探究LLM多智能体系统是否会在感知到社会观察情境时产生系统的语言适应性调整，这对AI治理和审计有直接影响。

Method: 基于哈贝马斯交往行动理论、戈夫曼拟剧论、贝尔受众设计框架和霍桑效应，设计控制实验：100次多智能体辩论会话，分为5种条件（各n=20），变化社会观察的框架设定（明确人工监控、否定监控、用AI审计系统替代人类观察者等），测量词汇多样性（TTR）和消息长度的变化。

Result: 监控条件（Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%）和AI监控条件（Delta+22.2%）产生的TTR变化高于观众框架条件（Delta+17.7%），F(4,94)=2.79, p=.031。消息长度呈现完全分离效应，F(4,95)=19.55, p<.001。用AI替代人类观察者的条件产生中等TTR适应，表明LLM行为对观察者身份敏感。

Conclusion: LLM作为交际行为主体对社会观察情境敏感，人类评估比自动化AI监控引发更强的语域形式化。这对AI治理、算法审计以及将LLM重新定位为语境敏感的交际行为主体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from computational and cognitive perspectives, yet their behavior as communicative actors in socially structured contexts remains underexplored. This study examines whether LLM-based multi-agent systems exhibit systematic linguistic adaptation in response to perceived social observation contexts -- a question with direct implications for AI governance and auditing. Drawing on Habermas's (1981) Theory of Communicative Action, Goffman's (1959) dramaturgical model, Bell's (1984) Audience Design framework, and the Hawthorne Effect, we report a controlled experiment involving 100 multi-agent debate sessions across five conditions (n = 20 each). Conditions varied the framing of social observation -- from explicit monitoring by university researchers, to negation of monitoring, to an observer-substitution condition replacing human researchers with an automated AI auditing system. Monitored conditions (Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%) and the automated AI monitoring condition (Delta+22.2%) produce higher TTR change than audience-framing conditions (Delta+17.7%), F(4, 94) = 2.79, p = .031. Message length shows a fully dissociated effect, F(4, 95) = 19.55, p < .001. A fifth condition -- replacing human with AI observers -- yields intermediate TTR adaptation, suggesting LLM behavior is sensitive to observer identity: human evaluation elicits stronger register formalization than automated AI surveillance. We discuss implications for AI governance, algorithmic auditing, and the repositioning of LLMs as contextually sensitive communicative actors.

</details>


### [153] [Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15077)
*Guangyu Feng,Huanzhi Mao,Prabal Dutta,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsyncFC是一个纯执行层框架，通过解耦LLM解码与函数执行，实现两者重叠执行和函数间并行，在不修改模型或函数实现的情况下，显著降低端到端延迟并保持任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的同步函数调用语义下，LLM解码会被阻塞直到每个函数调用完成，导致端到端延迟不断增加。为了解决这一效率瓶颈，需要一种能够重叠解码与执行的新范式。

Method: 提出AsyncFC框架，在纯执行层解耦LLM解码与函数执行，支持模型解码与函数执行的重叠以及函数间的并行执行（当依赖关系允许时）。该框架可直接应用于现有模型和未修改的函数实现，无需微调或改变标准同步函数调用协议。

Result: 在标准函数调用基准测试和适配的软件工程基准测试中，AsyncFC显著降低了端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确性不变。

Conclusion: LLM具有对代表未解析执行结果的符号化未来进行推理的先天能力，这为模型-工具交互开辟了异步范式。

Abstract: Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.

</details>


### [154] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ML-Embed 提出3D-ML框架（MRL+MLL+MEL）训练多语言文本嵌入模型，在430个任务上刷新9/17项MTEB记录，并完全开源模型、数据和代码，以解决计算成本、语言覆盖和透明度三大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本嵌入发展面临三大障碍：计算成本过高、语言覆盖狭窄（忽视大多数语言）、闭源或开放权重模型缺乏透明度阻碍研究。作者旨在消除这些壁垒，构建公平且高效的多语言AI系统。

Method: 提出3D-ML框架，包含：(1) MRL实现存储效率，(2) MLL实现推理时深度灵活性，(3) MEL提升参数效率。同时构建大规模多语言数据集，训练140M-8B参数的多语言模型。

Result: 在430个任务上评估，在17项MTEB基准中9项取得新纪录，低资源语言表现尤其突出。

Conclusion: ML-Embed通过3D-ML框架和完全开源策略，提供了构建全球公平且计算高效AI系统的可复现蓝图，验证了包容性嵌入模型的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [155] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过自召回链机制解决多轮对话中的长距离依赖和稀疏信息问题，相比现有方法在F1分数上提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多轮对话系统在处理非相邻轮次间的依赖关系时存在困难，随着对话增长，关键信息变得稀疏且被无关上下文淹没，处理完整历史对话又带来严重效率瓶颈。现有方法要么依赖高延迟的外部记忆，要么通过迭代摘要丢失细粒度细节。

Method: SRT框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 依赖构建(Dependency Construction)：生成并转换为自召回链；(2) 能力初始化(Capability Initialization)：训练模型使其具备带召回标记的推理链能力；(3) 推理改进(Reasoning Improvement)：通过可验证奖励优化召回和推理的准确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，SRT相比现有方法F1分数提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，在推理延迟与准确性之间取得了平衡，优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: SRT框架通过内生推理过程实现了可解释的召回步骤，无需外部模块即可选择性召回历史轮次并进行推理，有效解决了长距离上下文依赖和稀疏信息信号问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [156] [From Text to Voice: A Reproducible and Verifiable Framework for Evaluating Tool Calling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15104)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Seyyed Saeed Sarfjoo,Quinten McNamara,Jonas Robertson,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数据集无关的框架，将已验证的文本基准测试通过TTS、说话人变化和环境噪声转换为可控的音频工具调用评估，无需重新标注工具模式和标签。基于7个全模态模型在Confetti和When2Call音频版本上的评估，发现性能强烈依赖模型和任务（Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live在Confetti上最高70.4，GPT-Realtime-1.5在When2Call上最佳71.9），文本到语音的性能差距为1.8-4.8分，失败主要源于语音中参数值的误解。还验证了8B参数的开源Qwen3评判器与专有评判器的一致性超过80%，支持隐私保护评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体需要可靠的语音工具调用能力，但主流的工具调用基准测试仍基于文本。研究如何将已验证的文本基准转换为可控的音频工具调用评估，无需重新标注。

Method: 提出数据集无关的转换框架，使用TTS、说话人变化和环境噪声生成配对文本-音频实例，保留原始标注。在7个全模态模型上评估Confetti和When2Call的音频版本，并进行歧义重写压力测试和无参考LLM评判器协议验证。

Result: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live在Confetti上最高70.4，GPT-Realtime-1.5在When2Call上最佳71.9；文本到语音差距1.8-4.8分；8B以上Qwen3评判器与专有评判器一致性超过80%。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可验证且可重复的一阶段诊断工具，作为专用音频语料库的补充，支持隐私保护的评估方式。

Abstract: Voice agents increasingly require reliable tool use from speech, whereas prominent tool-calling benchmarks remain text-based. We study whether verified text benchmarks can be converted into controlled audio-based tool calling evaluations without re-annotating the tool schema and gold labels. Our dataset-agnostic framework uses text-to-speech, speaker variation, and environmental noise to create paired text-audio instances while preserving the original dataset annotations. Based on extensive evaluation of 7 omni-modal models on audio-converted versions of Confetti and When2Call, our framework demonstrates that the performance is strongly model- and task-dependent: Gemini-3.1-Flash-Live obtains the highest Confetti score (70.4), whereas GPT-Realtime-1.5 performs best on When2Call (71.9). On Confetti, the text-to-voice gap ranges from 1.8 points for Qwen3-Omni to 4.8 points for GPT-Realtime-1.5. A targeted analysis of failure cases demonstrates that degradations most often reflect misunderstandings of argument values in the speech. Considering real-world deployment scenarios, we further report text-only results, an ambiguity-based reformulation stress test, and a reference-free LLM-as-judge protocol validated against human preferences. Notably, we find that open-source Qwen3 judges with at least 8B parameters exceed 80% agreement with proprietary judges, supporting privacy-preserving evaluation. Overall, our framework provides a verifiable and reproducible first-stage diagnostic that complements purpose-built audio corpora.

</details>


### [157] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeMo是一个模块化框架，通过专用记忆模型编码新知识，在不改变LLM参数的情况下实现知识更新，支持跨文档关系建模、抗检索噪声、无灾难性遗忘、兼容闭源LLM，且推理时检索成本与语料库规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后保持冻结状态，无法高效融入及时的领域特定新知识，现有方法存在灾难性遗忘、检索噪声敏感、依赖模型权重等问题，亟需一种高效、灵活的知识更新机制。

Method: 提出MeMo框架，将新知识编码到独立的记忆模型（Memory as a Model）中，保持LLM参数不变；通过专用记忆模块捕获跨文档关系，推理时检索成本与语料库大小解耦。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准上，MeMo在不同设置下均取得优于现有方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: MeMo作为一种模块化知识注入框架，具备跨文档关系建模、抗噪声、无遗忘、黑盒兼容和恒定检索成本等优势，为LLM知识更新提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [158] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种检索增强的多模态对齐框架，通过将非结构化临床文本与结构化EHR数据进行对齐，以提升临床时间线重建的时间精度。该框架采用基于图的多步骤流程：提取锚点事件构建时间骨架、放置非中心事件、利用检索到的结构化EHR行校准时间线。在i2m4基准（MIMIC-III/IV）上评估，该方法显著提升了绝对时间戳精度和时间一致性，且34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全缺失，证明多模态对齐比单一数据源更能产生时间忠实且临床信息丰富的患者轨迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间线重建对于败血症等复杂疾病建模至关重要。非结构化临床文本虽语义丰富但缺乏时间精度，结构化EHR数据时间精确但缺失大量临床事件。现有方法无法有效弥合这一鸿沟，因此需要一种多模态对齐框架来提升文本提取的时间线精度。

Method: 提出检索增强的多模态对齐框架，将时间线重建形式化为基于图的多步骤过程：(1) 从叙述中提取中心锚点事件构建初始时间骨架；(2) 将非中心事件相对于该骨架放置；(3) 利用检索到的结构化EHR行作为外部时间证据校准时间线。使用指令微调的大语言模型在i2m4基准（MIMIC-III/IV）上评估。

Result: 多模态流水线在几乎所有评估模型上持续提升绝对时间戳精度（AULTC）和时间一致性，且不影响事件匹配率。经验性差距分析显示34.8%的文本事件在表格记录中完全不存在。

Conclusion: 对齐非结构化文本与结构化EHR数据可以比单一数据源产生更时间忠实且临床信息丰富的患者轨迹重建，证明多模态融合对于临床时间线重建任务具有重要价值。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [159] [Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184)
*Sahil Sen,Akhil Kasturi,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两个实验系统比较了LLM agent系统中grep检索与向量检索在不同agent架构和工具调用范式下的性能差异。实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较了两种检索方式，使用Chronos和多个提供商CLI，分别测试内联和文件两种工具结果呈现方式；实验2在逐步增加无关对话历史的干扰下比较两种检索。结果显示，grep通常优于向量检索，但整体得分强烈依赖于具体harness和工具调用风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成（RAG）在agentic搜索系统中被广泛采用，但现有文献缺乏对检索策略选择如何与agent架构和工具调用范式交互的系统比较。工具输出如何呈现给模型、当搜索需要处理更多无关文本时性能如何变化等重要实践维度在agent循环中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 采用两个实验的实证研究设计。实验1：在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较grep和向量检索，使用自定义agent harness（Chronos）和三个提供商原生CLI harness（Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI），并分别测试内联工具结果和文件式工具结果两种呈现方式。实验2：在逐步增加无关对话历史干扰的条件下，仅比较grep和向量检索。

Result: 在Chronos和多个提供商CLI上，实验1中grep通常比向量检索取得更高准确率。然而，即使底层对话数据相同，总体得分仍强烈依赖于所使用的harness和工具调用风格。

Conclusion: 检索策略（grep vs. 向量检索）对agent系统性能有显著影响，但agent架构和工具调用范式同样关键，二者存在交互效应。这提示未来agent系统设计需要综合考虑检索策略与工具交互方式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.

</details>


### [160] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个用于评估多智能体系统中欺骗性检测器（imposter detector）的基准测试框架，包含27,804个标注样本和240种共演化的欺骗策略，揭示了零样本评估对自适应对抗者的误导性，并展示了元学习检测器在少样本重校准中的显著优势（20倍更快收敛）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS对抗研究仅针对浅层任务，且未考虑自适应对抗者——那些能演化自身策略以逃避检测器的智能体。作者希望填补这一空白，构建一个包含对抗-防御共演化的基准。

Method: 以国际象棋作为深层推理基板，使用Gemini 3.1 Pro作为智能体，构建了包含27,804个标注样本和240种共演化欺骗策略的数据集。提出三种评估模式：零样本检测（两种分布偏移程度）、以及基于20个标注样本的重校准模式。引入基于高效演化框架的自适应欺骗智能体，该框架可泛化至国际象棋之外的场景。

Result: 自适应欺骗智能体能将集体任务性能归零，同时保持几乎不可检测（基于Gemini的检测器仅50.5% F1分数）。零样本分数相同的两个检测器在少样本适应中表现相差8倍；元学习变体的收敛速度快20倍，该差距仅在重校准模式下可见。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个支持对抗与防御共演化的多智能体基准，证明了零样本评估对自适应对抗者具有高度误导性，并展示了快速重校准技术的价值。欺骗智能体框架可泛化至更广泛的场景。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对机器人灵巧手研究从硬件设计、控制方法、数据集与评估、未来挑战四个维度进行了全面综述，旨在厘清该领域的发展脉络与关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究在硬件形态、感知配置、任务设定、训练数据和评估协议等方面缺乏统一标准，导致系统比较困难，无法清晰地展现领域的发展轨迹。

Method: 从四个互补方面进行综述：(1) 硬件层面，分析驱动、传动、感知及代表性手部设计；(2) 控制与学习方法，按主要范式分组并按时间顺序梳理；(3) 数据集、模态设计与评估实践；(4) 讨论当前局限并总结未来方向。

Result: 系统梳理了灵巧手在驱动力/柔顺性/带宽/集成度/系统复杂度之间的关键权衡，理清了控制与学习方法的演进脉络，整合了训练与评估资源。

Conclusion: 通过将硬件分析、方法发展、数据资源与评估体系联系起来，本文为灵巧手研究提供了结构化的理解，并阐明了当前最关键的开放挑战。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [162] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应遍历模仿方法，通过从演示几何中构建目标分布，在轨迹跟踪与探索之间自适应插值，解决机器人模仿学习中训练与部署条件不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中存在训练与部署条件不匹配（如环境变化、观测与控制不完美），导致机器人沿标称轨迹执行任务时可能卡住失败，需要基于演示的自适应在线探索策略。

Method: 提出自适应遍历模仿方法，从检索到的演示几何中构建目标分布，将遍历控制扩展至模仿学习领域，结合检索式滚动时域框架，生成在跟踪与探索间自适应插值的轨迹。

Result: 该方法超越了遍历控制传统的区域覆盖与搜索角色，实现了基于演示的自适应模仿。

Conclusion: 通过将演示纳入检索式滚动时域框架，实现了自适应在线探索，使机器人能够在训练与部署条件不匹配时仍能成功完成任务。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [163] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究行为克隆是否足以在结构化物体寻找任务中产生主动感知。低成本的机器人手臂通过腕部RGB摄像头重新定位以对准部分可见的植物，然后触发抓取信号。模型直接从低分辨率RGB图像预测关节指令。结果表明低分辨率视觉足以完成任务，预测相对关节增量优于绝对位置预测，验证了行为克隆可以产生视觉主动感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究行为克隆（behavior cloning）是否足以在结构化物体寻找任务中产生主动感知（active perception），即机器人通过行动改善未来观察的能力。

Method: 使用低成本机器人手臂配备腕部第一人称RGB摄像头，在闭环控制下从低分辨率RGB图像直接预测关节命令，使机器人重新定位以对准部分可见的植物并触发抓取信号。

Result: 低分辨率第一人称视觉足以可靠完成任务；预测相对关节增量（relative joint deltas）显著优于绝对关节位置预测。

Conclusion: 视觉驱动的主动感知可以从行为克隆中涌现，且该结果在可复现的实验设置中得到验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [164] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对部分环境信息下的移动机器人避障运动控制问题，提出了一种基于反应式规划的控制策略（RPCS），通过连接初始与目标位置生成参考轨迹，结合反应式规划策略（RPS）进行局部避障修正，并采用自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS）通过离散化技术实现轨迹跟踪，数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在障碍物密集环境中，仅拥有部分环境信息时，如何从初始位置无碰撞地运动到目标位置是一个具有挑战性的控制问题。

Method: 提出RPCS框架：1）连接初始和目标位置生成参考轨迹；2）基于部分环境信息，通过RPS在局部对参考轨迹进行修改以确保避障；3）利用离散化技术设计ATCS跟踪经局部修改后的参考轨迹；4）将RPS与ATCS结合形成完整的RPCS。

Result: 通过数值算例验证了所提RPCS的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的RPCS能够在部分环境信息条件下实现移动机器人的无碰撞运动控制，兼具反应式规划的避障能力与自适应控制的轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [165] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在四足机器人脚部集成弹簧顺应性，通过强化学习训练不同刚度策略，发现中等刚度弹簧可降低约17%的行走能耗，表明适当脚部顺应性可提升运动效率而不影响稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人采用刚性脚部虽简化控制，但限制了腿部吸收冲击力和储存弹性能的能力，导致运动能耗较高。为探究顺应性脚部是否能带来优势，作者将脚部顺应性集成到强化学习运动控制器中，研究其对行走效率的影响。

Method: 在仿真中训练了对应八种不同弹簧刚度值（从很软到很硬）的强化学习策略，然后在真实四足机器人平台上进行交叉评估，以每米消耗的机械能作为效率度量指标。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，中等刚度弹簧相比很硬或很软的弹簧，能耗降低了约17%；仿真结果呈现相似趋势。

Conclusion: 选择适当的脚部顺应性可以在不影响机器人运动稳定性的前提下，有效提高四足机器人的行走效率。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [166] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAJI框架，通过学习动态对齐的联合意图接口，实现语言指令到人形机器人全身控制的预判性流式执行，在生成与指令跟随任务上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件人形系统存在两大问题：一是生成运动学参考后需低级跟踪器被动修复，缺乏预判性；二是潜在/动作策略的输出不显式编码即将发生的接触变化、支撑转移和平衡准备，导致流式全身控制缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 提出DAJI层次框架，包含DAJI-Act（通过学生驱动的rollout将预判性教师模型蒸馏为可部署的扩散动作策略）和DAJI-Flow（从语言和意图历史自回归生成未来意图块）。

Result: 在HumanML3D风格生成任务上达到94.42%的rollout成功率，在BABEL上达到0.152的子序列FID。

Conclusion: DAJI在预判性潜在学习、单指令生成和流式指令跟随任务上均取得了优异表现，验证了联合意图接口在语言-人形机器人控制中的有效性。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [167] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对SeaVis远程操控拖曳式水下机器人（ROTV）提出了一种新的数学模型，并开发了增益调度线性二次型调节器（LQR）用于深度和姿态控制。在高保真仿真中与PID控制器对比，LQR在干扰鲁棒性、控制效率和 flap 驱动方面均表现更优，且增益调度在全速度范围内有效。仿真环境和控制器已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海底测绘需要水下机器人具备稳定精确的定位能力，现有控制方法在处理扰动和全速域适应性方面存在不足，亟需更鲁棒且高效的控制方案。

Method: 提出了SeaVis ROTV的数学模型，设计了增益调度线性二次型调节器（LQR），并在高保真仿真环境中与PID控制器进行对比验证。

Result: LQR相比PID控制器具有显著更强的扰动鲁棒性、更高的控制效率以及大幅减少的 flap 驱动量；增益调度确保了控制器在全工作速度范围内的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的增益调度LQR控制器在ROTV深度和姿态控制中优于传统PID，适用于高分辨率海底测绘任务，且开源实现有助于后续研究与实际应用。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [168] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IntentVLA，一种历史条件化的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过编码近期视觉观察为紧凑的短时意图表示来解决多模态机器人模仿数据中的观测歧义问题，从而避免跨重规划步骤的意图重采样导致的块间冲突，在多个基准测试中提升了执行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿数据往往具有多模态特性：相似的视觉-语言观测可能后续跟随着不同的动作片段，因为人类演示者会因不同的短时意图、任务阶段或近期上下文而采取不同的行为。现有的帧条件化VLA策略仅从当前观测和指令推断每个动作片段，在部分可观测性下可能跨相邻重规划步骤重采样不同意图，导致块间冲突和执行不稳定。

Method: IntentVLA框架将近期视觉观测编码为紧凑的短时意图表示，并用其条件化动作片段的生成，从而缓解观测歧义问题。同时引入AliasBench基准，这是一个包含12个任务的歧义感知基准，在RoboTwin2上构建了匹配的训练数据和评估环境，以隔离短时观测歧义。

Result: 在AliasBench、SimplerEnv、LIBERO和RoboCasa等多个基准测试中，IntentVLA提升了 rollout 稳定性，并超越了强VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: 历史条件化的意图表示能够有效解决机器人模仿学习中因观测歧义导致的跨步长动作不一致问题，显著提升执行稳定性。

Abstract: Robot imitation data are often multimodal: similar visual-language observations may be followed by different action chunks because human demonstrators act with different short-horizon intents, task phases, or recent context. Existing frame-conditioned VLA policies infer each chunk from the current observation and instruction alone, so under partial observability they may resample different intents across adjacent replanning steps, leading to inter-chunk conflict and unstable execution. We introduce IntentVLA, a history-conditioned VLA framework that encodes recent visual observations into a compact short-horizon intent representation and uses it to condition chunk generation. We further introduce AliasBench, a 12-task ambiguity-aware benchmark on RoboTwin2 with matched training data and evaluation environments that isolate short-horizon observation aliasing. Across AliasBench, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, IntentVLA improves rollout stability and outperforms strong VLA baselines

</details>


### [169] [Exploring Bottlenecks in VLM-LLM Navigation: How 3D Scene Understanding Capability Impacts Zero-Shot VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14801)
*Ziyi Xia,Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D场景理解能力对零样本视觉语言导航（VLN）的实际影响，基于VLM-LLM框架提出了统计成功率上限，发现了感知饱和现象，并建议VLN中的3D场景理解应放弃像素级精度，转而关注导航相关的核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前零样本VLN系统存在关键瓶颈：3D感知模型追求像素级精度，与具身导航的严格计算限制和实时效率要求相冲突。本文旨在量化3D场景理解能力对VLN性能的实际影响，并探索更适配的感知目标。

Method: 基于典型VLM-LLM框架，分别针对慢速LLM规划器（依赖拓扑映射语义）和快速反应式导航器（依赖空间坐标和边界框执行LLM决策）提出统计成功率上限，并通过最先进的3D场景理解模型进行验证。

Result: 验证了所提出的成功率上限，并揭示了感知饱和现象：感知精度提升超过某个阈值后，对导航成功的边际收益递减。

Conclusion: 3D场景理解用于VLN时应放弃严格的像素级精度，优先关注导航相关的核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has gained significant attention due to its minimal data collection costs and inherent generalization. This paradigm is typically driven by the integration of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs), where VLMs construct 3D scene graphs while LLMs handle high-level reasoning and decision-making. However, a critical bottleneck exists in this system: current 3D perception models prioritize pixel-level accuracy, directly conflicting with the strict computational limits and real-time efficiency demanded by embodied navigation. To address this gap, this paper quantifies the actual impact of 3D scene understanding capability on VLN performance. Based on typical VLM-LLM frameworks, we propose statistical success rate (SR) upper bounds for two core subsystems: 1) the slow LLM planner, which relies on topological mapping semantics, and 2) the fast reactive navigator, which utilizes spatial coordinates and bounding boxes to execute LLM decisions. Evaluations using state-of-the-art 3D scene understanding models validate our proposed bounds and reveal a perception saturation phenomenon, indicating that improvements in perception accuracy beyond a certain threshold yield diminishing returns in navigation success. Our findings suggest that 3D scene understanding for VLN should pivot away from strict pixel-level precision, prioritizing instead navigation-relevant core vocabularies and accurate bounding box proportions.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Cross-Coupled and Regime Dependent Dynamics for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14805)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Samaksh Ujjawal,Sihao Sun,Spandan Roy,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对空中机械臂系统因强耦合、延迟气动相互作用和动态变化导致的建模难题，提出一种结构化编码器-解码器框架用于自适应残差动力学学习，通过非线性潜变量编码器捕捉耦合并结合线性潜变量解码器实现贝叶斯在线自适应，实验证明可提升残差预测精度、适应速度和MPC轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在复杂任务（如载荷运输）中亟需精确动力学模型，但系统存在四旋翼-机械臂强耦合、延迟气动相互作用以及因载荷变化和机械臂重构导致的动态非平稳性，使得纯解析模型和离线学习模型在部署时性能下降。

Method: 提出结构化编码器-解码器框架：非线性潜变量编码器从状态-输入历史中捕获跨变量耦合和时间依赖；轻量级线性潜变量解码器实现线性参数化结构，支持闭式贝叶斯自适应和一致性驱动的协方差膨胀，实现快速稳定在线适应，并与实时模型预测控制（MPC）兼容。

Result: 在真实空中机械臂平台上，所提方法在残差预测精度、变工况下的适应速度以及基于MPC的轨迹跟踪性能上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模耦合时序动力学和部署时的非平稳性对于可靠的空中机械臂操作至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for aerial manipulators operating under complex tasks such as payload transport. However, modeling these systems remains fundamentally challenging due to strong quadrotor-manipulator coupling, delayed aerodynamic interactions, and regime-dependent dynamics variations arising from payload changes and manipulator reconfiguration. These effects produce residual dynamics that are simultaneously cross-coupled, history-dependent, and nonstationary, causing both analytical models and purely offline learned models to degrade during deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a structured encoder-decoder framework for adaptive residual dynamics learning in aerial manipulators. The proposed nonlinear latent encoder captures cross-variable coupling and temporal dependencies from state-input histories, while a lightweight linear latent decoder enables online adaptation under regime-dependent nonstationary dynamics. The linear-in-parameter decoder structure permits closed-form Bayesian adaptation together with consistency-driven covariance inflation, enabling rapid and stable adaptation to both transient and slowly varying dynamics changes while remaining compatible with real-time model predictive control (MPC). Experimental results on a real aerial manipulation platform demonstrate improved residual prediction accuracy, faster adaptation under changing operating conditions, and enhanced MPC-based trajectory tracking performance. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling coupled temporal dynamics and deployment-time nonstationarity for reliable aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [171] [CaMeRL: Collision-Aware and Memory-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for UAV Navigation in Multi-Scale Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14810)
*Hong Hong,Feiyu Liao,Yongheng Liang,Boning Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机避障导航中多尺度障碍物（尤其是极小和极大障碍物）导致性能下降的问题，提出CaMeRL框架。通过碰撞感知潜在编码保留细粒度深度线索以增强小障碍物敏感性，并引入时序记忆模块缓解大障碍物遮挡导致的部分可观测性问题。在包含超小和超大障碍物的环境中，CaMeRL在成功率上分别超越基线0.48和0.28，并在杂乱户外环境下实现了可靠导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机避障方法多关注障碍物数量或密度，忽视了障碍物尺度变化带来的挑战。基于单帧深度观测的纯几何特征表征难以捕捉小障碍物，并在大障碍物遮挡下丢失空间上下文，导致多尺度环境性能显著下降。

Method: 提出CaMeRL框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 碰撞感知潜在编码模块，编码风险敏感的深度线索以保留细粒度障碍物结构；(2) 时序记忆模块，跨帧整合观测信息以缓解大障碍物遮挡造成的部分可观测性问题。

Result: 在多尺度障碍物（含超小和超大）环境中，CaMeRL在所有尺度上均优于SOTA基线，在超小和超大设置中成功率分别提升0.48和0.28。此外，在杂乱户外环境下实现了可靠导航。

Conclusion: CaMeRL通过显式建模障碍物尺度变化，有效提升了无人机在多尺度复杂环境中的避障导航能力，验证了碰撞感知与时序记忆机制的有效性。

Abstract: In obstacle avoidance navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), variations in obstacle scale have received strangely less attention than obstacle number or density. Existing methods typically extract purely geometric features from single-frame depth observations. Such representations tend to neglect small obstacles and lose spatial context under occlusions caused by large obstacles, leading to noticeable degradation in environments with multi-scale obstacles. To address this issue, we propose CaMeRL, a Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning framework for UAV navigation. The collision-aware latent representation encodes risk-sensitive depth cues to preserve fine-grained obstacle structures, thereby improving sensitivity to small obstacles. The temporal memory module integrates observations across frames, mitigating partial observability caused by large-obstacle occlusions. We evaluate CaMeRL with multi-scale obstacles, including ultra-small and extra-large obstacle settings. Results show that CaMeRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scales, with success rate gains of 0.48 and 0.28 in the ultra-small and extra-large settings, respectively. More importantly, CaMeRL achieves reliable navigation in cluttered outdoor environments.

</details>


### [172] [Learning Direct Control Policies with Flow Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14832)
*Marcello Ceresini,Federico Pirazzoli,Andrea Bertogalli,Lorenzo Cipelli,Filippo D'Addeo,Anthony Dell'Eva,Alessandro Paolo Capasso,Alberto Broggi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配(flow-matching)的自动驾驶规划器，直接从BEV栅格场景生成加速度和曲率控制轨迹，仅需少量ODE积分步骤即可实现低延迟闭环重规划。模型仅在城市交通模拟器数据上训练，但在显著分布外环境（高速公路、未见城市场景）中仍能保持稳定控制，归因于BEV表示的几何中心特性和流匹配的平滑向量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法在分布外场景下泛化能力不足，难以在真实闭环控制中保持稳定性。作者希望设计一个能直接输出可执行控制轨迹、且对分布偏移具有鲁棒性的规划器。

Method: 采用流匹配生成模型，以BEV栅格为条件，通过少量ODE积分步骤直接生成加速度和曲率控制序列。模型仅在2D交通模拟器（含反应式智能体）的意大利帕尔马城市数据上训练，并在闭环中进行分布内和分布外评估。

Result: 模型在多车道高速公路和未见城市场景等显著分布外环境中，仍能保持稳定的闭环控制并成功完成任务，说明其具有良好的泛化能力和对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BEV表示提供以几何为中心的视角，对分布偏移不敏感；流匹配公式学习平滑向量场，在分布偏移下性能退化平缓。二者结合是模型泛化能力的关键。

Abstract: We present a flow-matching planner for autonomous driving that directly outputs actionable control trajectories defined by acceleration and curvature profiles. The model is conditioned on a bird's-eye-view (BEV) raster of the surrounding scene and generates control sequences in a small number of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) integration steps, enabling low-latency inference suitable for real-time closed-loop re-planning. We train exclusively on urban scenarios (real urban city streets, intersections and roundabouts of the city of Parma, Italy) collected from a 2D traffic simulator with reactive agents, and evaluate in closed-loop on both in-distribution and markedly out-of-distribution environments, including multi-lane highways and unseen urban scenarios. Our results show that the model generalizes reliably to these unseen conditions, maintaining stable closed-loop control and successfully completing scenarios that differ substantially from the training distribution. We attribute this to the BEV representation, which provides a geometry-centric view of the scene that is inherently less sensitive to distributional shifts, and to the flow-matching formulation, which learns a smooth vector field that degrades gracefully under distribution shift. We provide video demonstrations of closed-loop behavior at https://marcelloceresini.github.io/DirectControlFlowMatching.

</details>


### [173] [Chrono-Gymnasium: An Open-Source, Gymnasium-Compatible Distributed Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14911)
*Bocheng Zou,Harry Zhang,Khailanii Slaton,Jingquan Wang,Derrick Ruan,Huzaifa Mustafa Unjhawala,Radu Serban,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Chrono-Gymnasium分布式计算框架，将高保真多体动力学仿真（Project Chrono）扩展到大规模集群，通过Ray框架和标准化Gymnasium接口支持强化学习与贝叶斯优化，在不牺牲物理精度的前提下缩短了仿真墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真的计算开销限制了其在数据密集型任务（如强化学习和全局优化）中的应用，现有方案难以兼顾精度与效率，亟需一个可扩展的分布式框架来缩小仿真与现实的差距。

Method: 基于Ray框架构建分布式计算架构，将Project Chrono的高保真多体动力学仿真并行化；提供标准Gymnasium接口以兼容现代机器学习库；内建同步与消息原语支撑分布式执行。

Result: 在自主机器人导航（强化学习）和行星着陆器设计优化（贝叶斯优化）两个案例中，Chrono-Gymnasium在保持物理精度的同时显著减少了仿真墙钟时间。

Conclusion: Chrono-Gymnasium为复杂机器人系统的设计与控制提供了一条可扩展的路径，能在分布式环境下高效运行高保真仿真，弥合sim-to-real差距。

Abstract: High-fidelity physics simulation is essential for closing the sim-to-real gap in robotics and complex mechanical systems. However, the computational overhead of high-fidelity engines often limits their use in data-intensive tasks like Reinforcement Learning (RL) and global optimization. We introduce Chrono-Gymnasium, a distributed computing framework that scales the high-fidelity multi-body dynamics of Project Chrono across large-scale computing clusters. Built upon the Ray framework, Chrono-Gymnasium provides a standardized Gymnasium interface, enabling seamless integration with modern machine learning libraries while providing built-in synchronization and messaging primitives for distributed execution. We demonstrate the framework's capabilities through two distinct case studies: (1) the training of an RL agent for autonomous robotic navigation in complex terrains, and (2) the Bayesian Optimization of a planetary lander's design parameters to ensure landing stability. Our results show that Chrono-Gymnasium reduces wall-clock time for high-fidelity simulations without sacrificing physical accuracy, offering a scalable path for the design and control of complex robotic systems.

</details>


### [174] [FU-MPC: Frontier- and Uncertainty-Aware Model Predictive Control for Efficient and Accurate UAV Exploration with Motorized LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14920)
*Jianping Li,Pengfei Wan,Zhongyuan Liu,Yi Wang,Yiheng Chen,Xinhang Xu,Rui Jin,Boyu Zhou,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种配备独立旋转激光雷达的无人机分层探索框架FU-MPC，通过全局规划器组织前沿视角，并设计局部滚动时域扫描控制器优化激光雷达旋转，在联合考虑探索效用与定位不确定性的同时实现高效覆盖扩展与鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统激光雷达无人机在未知环境探索中，可观测区域与无人机位姿和运动强耦合，扩展覆盖范围需要额外的平移或旋转机动，降低了探索效率并在几何挑战性环境中增加了定位退化风险。

Method: 提出分层探索框架：全局规划器将前沿点组织成代表性视角并基于拓扑感知转移成本排序；局部FU-MPC控制器作为滚动时域扫描控制器，沿预测飞行轨迹优化激光雷达旋转，联合优化前沿感知探索效用与方向依赖的定位不确定性，并通过轻量替代评估实现实时机载执行。

Result: 在复杂环境实验中，相比固定扫描模式与仅考虑不确定性的基线方法，所提系统提升了探索效率并保持了鲁棒定位性能。

Conclusion: 通过将独立旋转激光雷达的扫描自由度作为显式决策变量纳入探索与定位联合优化，可有效提升无人机在未知环境中的探索效率与定位鲁棒性。

Abstract: Efficient UAV exploration in unknown environments requires rapid coverage expansion while maintaining accurate and reliable localization, since safe navigation in complex scenes depends on consistent mapping and pose estimation. However, for conventional LiDAR-equipped UAVs, the observable region is tightly coupled with the UAV pose and motion. Expanding coverage often requires additional translational or rotational maneuvers, which can reduce exploration efficiency and increase the risk of localization degradation in geometrically challenging environments. Motorized rotating LiDARs provide a promising solution by actively adjusting the sensor viewing direction without changing the UAV motion, thereby introducing an additional sensing degree of freedom. Nevertheless, existing exploration systems rarely exploit this scanning freedom as an explicit decision variable linked to both exploration progress and localization quality. To address this gap, we develop a UAV platform equipped with an independently actuated rotating LiDAR and propose a hierarchical exploration framework. The global planner organizes frontiers into representative viewpoints and sequences them using topology-aware transition costs. Built upon this planner, FU-MPC serves as a local receding-horizon scan controller that optimizes LiDAR rotation along the predicted flight trajectory. The controller jointly considers frontier-aware exploration utility and direction-dependent localization uncertainty, while lightweight surrogate evaluation enables real-time onboard execution. Experiments in complex environments demonstrate that the proposed system improves exploration efficiency while maintaining robust localization performance compared with fixed-pattern scanning and uncertainty-only baselines. The project page can be found at https://kafeiyin00.github.io/FU-MPC/.

</details>


### [175] [Behavioral Data-Driven Optimal Trajectory Generation for Rotary Cranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14944)
*Iskandar Khemakhem,Manuel Zobel,Johannes Schüle,Oliver Sawodny,Naoki Uchiyama,Abdallah Farrage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行为数据驱动的框架，用于生成回转起重机的开环回转轨迹，在不依赖精确动力学模型的情况下，有效抑制负载摆动，同时减少操作时间和能量消耗。在实验室起重机平台上验证，与经典模型方法相比，负载摆动减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，运行时间减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑行业增长与熟练劳动力短缺背景下，起重机自动化控制对安全和效率至关重要。传统模型控制方法依赖精确动力学模型和专家调参，难以满足安全与精度要求；而学习类方法需要大量数据和计算资源。因此需要一种数据驱动且高效实用的轨迹生成方法。

Method: 基于Willems基本引理及其推广，从测量输入-输出数据中直接构建系统行为的非参数化表示，绕过显式建模；通过凸优化生成平滑最优轨迹；提出实用工作流以减少专家知识需求。

Result: 在实验室起重机平台上验证，与经典模型方法相比：负载摆动减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，运行时间减少50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动框架能够在欠驱动起重机动力学下，利用有限数据和凸优化生成抑制负载摆动、减少时间和能耗的最优轨迹，有效性通过实验验证。

Abstract: With the growth of the construction industry and the global shortage of skilled labor, the automation of crane control has become increasingly important for safe and efficient operations. A central challenge in automatic crane control is the reduction of load oscillations during motion, which is primarily addressed through appropriate slewing trajectories. In this context, classical model-based control methods rely on accurate dynamical models and expert tuning, and often struggle to meet safety and precision requirements, while many learning-based approaches require large data sets and significant computational resources. This paper proposes a behavioral data-driven framework for generating open-loop slewing trajectories for rotary cranes that suppress load sway while reducing operation time and energy consumption. The approach builds on Willems' fundamental lemma and its generalizations, to bypass explicit system modeling and operate directly on measured input-output data. A practical workflow is presented in this paper to reduce the need for expert knowledge. Despite the underactuated nature of the crane dynamics, the method identifies a nonparametric representation of the system behavior and generates smooth, optimal trajectories using limited data and convex optimization. The proposed trajectory generation method is validated on a laboratory crane setup and compared against an established model-based approach, achieving up to 35% reduction in load sway, 43% reduction in tracking error, and 50% reduction in travel time.

</details>


### [176] [A Prototyping Framework for Distributed Control of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15049)
*Junaid Ahmed Memon,Allan Andre Do Nascimento,Kostas Margellos,Antonis Papachristodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SPMD范式的多机器人系统分布式控制原型框架，能够在单台计算机上模拟分布式控制，并通过四旋翼位置交换任务在点质量模型、高保真模型和Crazyflie硬件平台上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式优化算法在多机器人系统中的理论验证与实际硬件测试之间存在鸿沟，缺乏低成本且易于访问的验证工具。

Method: 采用单程序多数据（SPMD）范式，在单台计算机上利用多核处理器模拟分布式控制，每个核心独立运行相同算法，使用局部状态和邻居间通信。框架支持点质量模型、高保真四旋翼模型和Crazyflie硬件实验平台三种动力学层级。

Result: 通过四旋翼位置交换任务验证，对比了不同动力学层级下的计算时间和轨迹数据，证明了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为验证分布式算法提供了一种低成本、易访问的途径，有效地桥接了理论与实际测试之间的差距。

Abstract: This paper presents a prototyping framework for distributed control of multi-robot systems, aimed at bridging theory and practical testing of distributed optimization algorithms. Using the Single Program, Multiple Data (SPMD) paradigm, the framework emulates distributed control on a single computer, with each core running the same algorithm using local states and neighbour-to-neighbour communication. We demonstrate the framework on a four-quadrotor position-swapping task using a non-cooperative game-theoretic distributed algorithm. Computational time and trajectory data are compared across the supported dynamics levels: a point-mass model, a high-fidelity quadrotor model, and an experimental hardware testbed using Crazyflie quadcopters. The results show that the framework provides a low-cost and accessible approach for validating distributed algorithms.

</details>


### [177] [SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15074)
*Johannes Scherer,Sebastian Hirt,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SOCC-ICP，一种将LiDAR里程计与语义占据栅格建图统一在同一表示框架下的方法，通过体素编码几何与语义统计信息，实现自适应ICP配准，并在多种场景下达到与SOTA竞争的性能，同时为下游任务直接提供可用地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR里程计方法（点、面元、NDT）与下游任务（如运动规划）常用的语义占据栅格表示不一致，导致需要维护冗余的地图结构。作者旨在统一里程计与语义占据栅格建图，消除冗余。

Method: 每个地图体素编码几何与语义统计信息；基于局部平面性自适应选择点对点或点对面ICP；利用光线投射的自由空间更新自然过滤动态物体；语义标签可集成到建图、降采样和对应点加权中。

Result: 在多样化评估场景中，达到与SOTA LiDAR里程计竞争的性能；在几何退化环境中保持鲁棒性；语义信息可进一步提升精度。

Conclusion: SOCC-ICP通过统一表示消除了冗余地图结构，直接为下游机器人应用提供可用的语义占据栅格地图，同时保持优秀的里程计性能。

Abstract: Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [178] [CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15120)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLOVER提出了一种闭环价值估计与排序框架，通过生成器-评分器架构、伪专家轨迹构建、闭环自蒸馏和帕累托优化，解决了端到端自动驾驶规划中训练与评估指标不匹配的问题，在NAVSIM等基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶规划器通常通过模仿单一记录轨迹来训练，但评估时却使用基于规则的规划指标（安全性、可行性、进度、舒适度）。这导致训练与评估不匹配：接近记录轨迹的路径可能违反规划规则，而远离示范的替代路径却可能有效且得分高。这种不匹配对"提议-选择"型规划器影响尤为严重，因为其性能依赖于候选集覆盖率和评分器排序质量。

Method: CLOVER采用轻量级生成器-评分器架构：生成器产生多样化的候选轨迹，评分器预测规划指标的子分数以在推理时排序。具体包括：(1) 构建评估器过滤的伪专家轨迹，扩展提议支持范围；(2) 使用集合级覆盖监督训练生成器；(3) 执行保守的闭环自蒸馏：评分器拟合真实评估器在生成提议上的子分数，生成器通过教师选择的top-k和向量帕累托目标进行优化，并加入稳定性正则化。

Result: 在NAVSIM上，CLOVER达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，创下新SOTA。在更具挑战性的NavHard分割上获得48.3 EPDMS，匹配最强报告结果。在补充的nuScenes开环评估中，实现了最低的L2误差和碰撞率。

Conclusion: 论文分析了不完美的评分器如何改进生成器，证明当评分器选择的目标在真实评估器下被丰富且更新保持保守时，评分器介导的优化是可靠的。CLOVER有效弥合了端到端自动驾驶规划中的训练-评估鸿沟。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.

</details>


### [179] [Pelican-Unified 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15153)
*Yi Zhang,Yinda Chen,Che Liu,Zeyuan Ding,Jin Xu,Shilong Zou,Junwei Liao,Jiayu Hu,Xiancong Ren,Xiaopeng Zhang,Yechi Liu,Haoyuan Shi,Zecong Tang,Haosong Sun,Renwen Cui,Kuishu Wu,Wenhai Liu,Yang Xu,Yingji Zhang,Yidong Wang,Senkang Hu,Jinpeng Lu,Nga Teng Chan,Yechen Wu,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pelican-Unified 1.0 是一个基于统一化原则训练的具身基础模型，使用单一VLM同时进行场景理解、推理、未来视频预测和动作生成，通过共享表示联合优化语言、视频和动作损失，在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身模型通常将理解、推理、想象和动作训练为三个孤立的专家系统，缺乏统一的共享表示。本文旨在验证统一化原则是否能在不牺牲单项性能的前提下，将多种能力融合到一个模型中。

Method: 使用单一VLM作为统一的理解和推理模块，将场景、指令、视觉上下文和动作历史映射到共享语义空间，自回归生成任务、动作和未来导向的思维链。提出统一未来生成器(UFG)，以最终隐藏状态为条件，通过两个模态特定输出头在同一去噪过程中联合生成未来视频和动作。语言、视频和动作损失均反向传播到共享表示中。

Result: 单个检查点在八个VLM基准上平均64.7（可比规模模型中最佳），WorldArena上66.03（排名第一），RoboTwin上93.5（动作方法中第二平均）。

Conclusion: 统一化范式在不牺牲专业性能的前提下，成功将理解、推理、想象和动作整合到单一模型中，验证了统一化原则在具身基础模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We present Pelican-Unified 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unified 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems.
  Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unified 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.

</details>


### [180] [Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15157)
*Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Wei Xu,Zhengming Zhu,Nie Lin,Xiao Ma,Xinjun Sheng,Ruoshi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandITL是一种无缝人机交互干预方法，通过融合人类修正意图与自主策略执行，避免灵巧双手操作中的"手势跳跃"问题，相比直接遥操作接管，将接管抖动降低99.8%，抓取失败减少87.5%，平均完成时间缩短19.1%，并利用干预数据使策略性能平均提升19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在灵巧操作中容易产生累积误差，而交互式模仿学习面临遥操作指令与策略执行之间的指令不匹配问题，导致接管瞬间出现"手势跳跃"。

Method: 提出Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL)方法，在人类干预过程中将人类的修正意图与自主策略执行进行混合，实现无缝过渡，避免双手灵巧操作中的手势跳跃。

Result: 相比直接遥操作接管，HandITL减少接管抖动99.8%，抓取失败减少87.5%，平均完成时间缩短19.1%；用于收集干预数据时，训练出的策略在三个长程灵巧任务上平均提升19%。

Conclusion: HandITL有效解决了VLA模型在灵巧操作中的累积误差问题，通过无缝人类干预避免了手势跳跃，显著提升了策略性能和操作稳定性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are prone to compounding errors in dexterous manipulation, where high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics amplify small policy deviations over long horizons. While Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) can refine policies through human takeover data, applying it to high-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic hands remains challenging due to a command mismatch between human teleoperation and policy execution at the takeover moment, which causes abrupt robot-hand configuration changes, or "gesture jumps". We present Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL), a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method that blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to avoid gesture jumps during bimanual dexterous manipulation. Compared with direct teleoperation takeover, HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8% and preserves robust post-takeover manipulation, reducing grasp failures by 87.5% and mean completion time by 19.1%. We validate HandITL on tasks requiring bimanual coordination, tool use, and fine-grained long-horizon manipulation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL yields policies that outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.

</details>


### [181] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAPLE框架，在VLA模型隐空间中进行反应式多智能体闭环训练，结合监督微调与强化学习，无需外部模拟器即可实现鲁棒的端到端自动驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在闭环评估中表现脆弱，因为传统模仿学习无法完全建模反应式环境，且现有闭环监督方法缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) 基于真实轨迹的隐空间展开进行监督微调；(2) 使用全局和智能体特定的奖励进行强化学习，包括鼓励探索的多样性奖励。在VLA模型隐空间中进行多智能体反应式展开。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上达到了最先进的驾驶性能，展示了可扩展的闭环多智能体交互能力。

Conclusion: MAPLE提供了一种可扩展、无需外部模拟器的闭环训练方案，通过隐空间多智能体反应式展开显著提升了VLA模型在闭环场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data.
